# Licence for documentation and original creative assets

Unless a file says otherwise, the following parts of KTBT are licensed under the
**Creative Commons Attribution-ShareAlike 4.0 International** licence (CC BY-SA 4.0):

- documentation (`*.md` files, except the licence files themselves)
- original art, audio, music and writing made for this project, including the
  K-drama style guide and the AI prompt text

Full licence text: https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/4.0/legalcode

You are free to share and adapt this material for any purpose, including commercially,
as long as you **give appropriate credit** and **distribute your contributions under the
same licence**.

The placeholder sounds and the Arirang arrangement are made in code (`scripts/sound/ambience.gd`,
`scripts/sound/music.gd`) and are original to this project. Arirang itself is a traditional Korean
folk song in the public domain.

## Fonts (`fonts/`)

| File | What | Source | Licence |
|---|---|---|---|
| `fonts/galmuri/Galmuri14.ttf`, `Galmuri11.ttf`, `Galmuri9.ttf`, `Galmuri7.ttf` | Galmuri, a pixel font for Hangul and Latin, by Lee Minseo (v2.40.4): the words in the bar's art (the sign, the neon, the CLOSED board) | https://github.com/quiple/galmuri | SIL Open Font License 1.1 (`fonts/galmuri/OFL.txt`, which travels with the fonts) |
